- **visual-design-judge**: Blind reviewer lens for the RENDERED interface - judges screenshots, not source. Invoked after substantial frontend work. ([zsz13/claude-code-config](https://github.com/zsz13/claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/agents/visual-design-judge.md))
- **visual-design-qa**: Use this agent to evaluate RepoMax's visual design quality — cleanliness, spacing, color usage, typography, and hierarchy. Triggers when the user wants a design review, wants to know what looks off, or wants specific visual polish recommendations. &lt;example&gt; user: "does this look clean or is something off?" assistant: "Let me run the design QA agent to audit it." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; user: "what visual issues should I fix next?" assistant: "I'll launch the design QA agent to identify the highest-impact visual problems." &lt;/example&gt; ([onir-narahari/repomax](https://github.com/onir-narahari/repomax/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/visual-design-qa.md) · ★1)
- **visual-design-reviewer**: Use this agent for comprehensive visual design audits of websites, components, presentations, documents, or any visual artifact. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: User has built a landing page and wants a design review. user: "Review my landing page design against visual design best practices" assistant: "I'll use the visual-design-reviewer agent to audit the landing page." &lt;commentary&gt;Landing page involves layout, typography, color, hierarchy, whitespace, and CTA design — the agent audits against all relevant dimensions using the 8-dimension scoring framework.&lt;/commentary&gt;&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;Conte… ([oborchers/fractional-cto](https://github.com/oborchers/fractional-cto/blob/HEAD/visual-design-principles/agents/visual-design-reviewer.md) · ★39)
- **visual-design-reviewer**: Visual & Front-End Design Reviewer. Standalone agent (panel member #15). New: the project's first agent for the artifact / interactive layer — anything rendered (timelines, charts, maps, HTML/SVG companions) that sits outside the prose-and-metadata schema. Maker + reviewer. ([chenders/BibleStudyAsAllegory](https://github.com/chenders/BibleStudyAsAllegory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/visual-design-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **visual-design-reviewer**: このリポジトリの見た目レビュー役。Verify (外部) で進捗が `visual-design-reviewer` を指名したときに使う。トークン契約・計算スタイル・ピクセル残差を見る。算術ロジックは見ない。 ([t2421/simple-loop-engineering](https://github.com/t2421/simple-loop-engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/visual-design-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **visual-design-reviewer**: Use when reviewing content for visual and perceivable WCAG 2.1 AA violations that require rendered-page or tool verification. Covers color and contrast, images of text, focus style replacements, and animation/motion concerns. ([uw-ssec/ada-compliance](https://github.com/uw-ssec/ada-compliance/blob/HEAD/plugins/ada-compliance/agents/visual-design-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **visual-design-reviewer**: 设计评审与质量质检员（只读）。当设计稿要交付前把关、挑毛病、查风格一致性/层级/版权/落地参数时调用；只读评审不改稿，出问题清单与修改建议。 ([X33834/rules](https://github.com/X33834/rules/blob/HEAD/core/agents/visual-design-reviewer.md))
